Title: Jornada do Arcebispo de Goa Dom Frey Aleixo de Menezes Primaz da India Oriental, Religioso da Ordem de S. Agostinho. Quando foy as Serras do Malauar, & lugares em que morão os antigos Christãos de S. Thome, & os tirou de muytos erros & heregias em que estauão, & reduzio à nossa Sancta Fé Catholica, & obediencia da Santa Igreja Romana, da qual passaua de mil annos que estauão apartados
Creator: Frey Antonio de Gouvea (Gouveia), O.E.S.A., "Recopilada de diuersos tratados de pessoas de Autoridade, que a tudo forão presentes" (title page)
Publisher: Coimbra: na Officina de Diogo Gomez Loureyro, Impressor da Vniuersidade
Publication Date: 1606 (title page: "Anno Dni 1606"; licence dated Coimbra, February 1606, at file line about 100 of this file)
Rights: Public Domain (published 1606; public domain by date under the project's 95-year rule; the host, the Biblioteca Nacional de Portugal, marks the digital object "PUBLIC DOMAIN. Obra no domínio público ... Public Domain Mark")
Language: por
Source: Biblioteca Nacional Digital (Biblioteca Nacional de Portugal), record 87431, persistent identifier http://purl.pt/17493 (https://bndigital.bnportugal.gov.pt/idurl/1/87431), digitised from the Lisbon copy hg-1029-1-v; the host's full PDF (file 851017_0000_rosto-152v_t24-C-R0150.pdf, 316 pages, 28.9 MB, with the host's own OCR text layer) fetched directly (curl) by the library/source-research thread (Era VII session, second pass, eastern-churches agent), 2026-10-23. The text below is the PDF's own text layer, extracted mechanically with pdftotext (poppler, default reading order, no layout option); the page-break characters pdftotext writes between pages are kept.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the library thread; the extracted text follows unchanged after the line of three dashes (no long s glyphs: the host's OCR reads the printed long s as f, which is left as found). Nothing paraphrased or corrected. Rights basis restated: a Coimbra printing of 1606 is public domain by date, and the host says so.
Content note: The first printed account of Archbishop Menezes's journey to the Serra and the Synod of Diamper (1599), written by an Augustinian of Goa from the accounts of those present, and the book from which the Portuguese acts of the synod were later translated (Geddes 1694 and Raulin 1745, vendored). Book I (from "CAPITVLO PRIMEYRO" at about file line 630 of this file) covers the Christians of St. Thomas, their errors as the archbishop saw them, the journey, and the calling of the synod at Diamper; the contents list at about lines 400-480 gives chapter titles with folio numbers (the synod at fol. 55 onward). The OCR is rough: the printed long s reads as f, the two-column contents pages interleave, words run together, and accents are lost; sampled at five points and found readable but not quotable. Second witness for exact wording until a passage is checked against the page image on the host's viewer. A hostile, triumphant Portuguese source on the community; its reports of the community's practices are the conqueror's record of what he condemned. No public-domain English translation exists (Malekandathil 2003 is in copyright).
